- **gnome**: Worker agent that builds artifacts from explicit plans. This agent should be invoked when another agent says "delegate to gnome", "spawn a gnome", "have a gnome build this", or when a parent agent (Scribe or Familiar) needs isolated execution of a clearly-defined build task.
